--- Commit Message ---
LU-20438 clio: Enable P2P DMA in client Direct I/O path

Enable PCI P2P DMA in the client Direct I/O path. Use the
new page extraction wrapper to handle P2P DMA pages
correctly during DIO read/write operations.

Optimized struct cl_dio_pages by packing cdp_page_count (24 bits)
and cdp_pinned (1 bit) into a single word.
Fixed refcount underflow by avoiding put_page for non-pinned pages.

Signed-off-by: Shivaji Kant <shivajikant@google.com>

--- Message (Gerrit AI review for Lustre, 2026-07-27 20:13:20.000000000) ---
Patch Set 17:

(13 comments)

AI code review results below, please doublecheck

Experimental feature testing in progress, please pay attention to the comments

Some concerns, see inline comments. Two of them look like functional regressions rather than nits: the new OBD_BRW_RDMA_ONLY gate in osc_dio_submit() also catches nvidia-fs/GDS pages, and ci_p2pdma is merged across OSCs with OR instead of AND, which re-opens the mixed IB/TCP case this patch is meant to close.

There is no test coverage for the new behaviour anywhere in the series - a sanity-dio case that exercises aligned DIO on a P2P-incapable network (so it takes the new -EOPNOTSUPP path) would be worth adding.

The new exported LNet API (LNetRouteP2PCapable() plus its api.h declaration) might sit better in the "LU-20438 lnet: Implement LNet support for PCI P2P DMA" patch of this series, so the LNet interface lands with the rest of the LNet work.
